  window.__oneTrustBoot = { mode: 'anonymous' };

  try {
    var raw = window.localStorage.getItem('oneTrustCrossDeviceConsent');
    if (!raw) return;

    var cache = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!cache || !cache.uid) return;

    // Cross-device consent was recently reported disabled for this user: stay anonymous, but record
    // the uid so the auth sync hook knows there is nothing to reconcile and skips its token requests.
    if (cache.disabled === true) {
      if (typeof cache.checkedAt === 'number' && Date.now() / 1000 < cache.checkedAt + 3600) {
        window.__oneTrustBoot = { mode: 'anonymous', uid: cache.uid };
      }
      return;
    }

    if (!cache.identifier || !cache.token) return;

    var parts = cache.token.split('.');
    if (parts.length < 2) return;

    var base64 = parts[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
    var padded = base64.padEnd(base64.length + ((4 - (base64.length % 4)) % 4), '=');
    var payload = JSON.parse(atob(padded));
    if (typeof payload.exp !== 'number') return;
    if (Date.now() / 1000 >= payload.exp - 60) return;

    // Assigned (not `var`-declared) so the property stays configurable and teardown can delete it.
    window.OneTrust = {
      dataSubjectParams: { id: cache.identifier, isAnonymous: false, token: cache.token }
    };
    window.__oneTrustBoot = { mode: 'authenticated', uid: cache.uid };
  } catch (error) {
    // Never let consent bootstrapping break page parse; anonymous is the safe default.
  }
})();</script><script id="one-trust-consent-shim" type="text/javascript" data-nscript="beforeInteractive">window.__oneTrustConsentQueue = [];
